ADVANCED GAMIFICATION\\\\n\\\\nBeyond badges \\\\u2014 gamification that drives real behavioral change:\\\\n\\\\n### For Clients:\\\\n\\\\n**Levels System:**\\\\n- Level 1: Newcomer (completed intake)\\\\n- Level 2: Committed (completed first diagnostic)\\\\n- Level 3: In Motion (completed 3 sessions + 5 tasks)\\\\n- Level 4: Gaining Traction (BHS improved 10+ points)\\\\n- Level 5: Transforming (BHS improved 20+ points)\\\\n- Level 6: High Performer (BHS above 75)\\\\n- Level 7: Elite (BHS above 85 for 3+ months)\\\\n- Level 8: Champion (BHS above 90 + refers another client)\\\\n\\\\nEach level unlocks:\\\\n- A visual badge on their dashboard\\\\n- A shareable achievement graphic\\\\n- Access to progressively advanced resources\\\\n- At Level 5+: invitation to join a peer group of similar-level clients\\\\n\\\\n**Streaks:**\\\\n- Weekly pulse check-in streak: \\\\\\\"12 weeks in a row! \\\\ud83d\\\\udd25\\\\\\\"\\\\n- Task completion streak: \\\\\\\"Perfect month \\\\u2014 all tasks completed on time!\\\\\\\"\\\\n- Session attendance streak: \\\\\\\"6 consecutive sessions attended\\\\\\\"\\\\n- Broken streaks trigger gentle re-engagement: \\\\\\\"You missed your check-in this week. Take 30 seconds to get back on track.\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n**Challenges:**\\\\n- Coach assigns a 30-day challenge: \\\\\\\"Delegate one decision per day for 30 days\\\\\\\"\\\\n- Client checks in daily\\\\n- Completion = badge + highlight in next session prep\\\\n- Peer group challenges: \\\\\\\"Which group member can improve their Financial score the most in 90 days?\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n### For Coaches:\\\\n\\\\n**Coaching Milestones:**\\\\n- 10th client onboarded\\\\n- 100th session completed\\\\n- 50th diagnostic run\\\\n- First client reaches BHS 80+\\\\n- 5 clients with positive ROI verified\\\\n- 1,000 coaching hours logged\\\\n\\\\n**Leaderboard (opt-in, anonymized):**\\\\n- \\\\\\\"Your average client BHS improvement: 18 points (top 25% of BoldEdge coaches)\\\\\\\"\\\\n- \\\\\\\"Your client retention rate: 88% (top 15%)\\\\\\\"\\\\n- \\\\\\\"Your task completion rate: 85% (above average)\\\\\\\"\\\\n- Not competitive \\\\u2014 more like \\\\\\\"here's where you stand\\\\\\\" benchmarking\\\\n\\\\n---\\\\n\\\\n## 9. FINANCIAL MODELING SUITE (For Business-Focused Coaching)\\\\n\\\\nBeyond the cash runway calculator \\\\u2014 a light financial modeling layer:\\\\n\\\\n### Break-Even Calculator:\\\\n- Inputs: Fixed costs, variable costs, average revenue per unit\\\\n- Output: \\\\\\\"You need 47 customers to break even. You currently have 38.\\\\\\\"\\\\n- Scenario: \\\\\\\"If you raise prices 15%, breakeven drops to 41 customers\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n### Hiring ROI Calculator:\\\\n- \\\\\\\"If you hire a salesperson at $65K + $20K commission potential...\\\\\\\"\\\\n- \\\\\\\"They need to generate $127K in revenue to be ROI-positive\\\\\\\"\\\\n- \\\\\\\"Based on your current close rate of 22%, they need 48 leads/month\\\\\\\"\\\\n- Connected to diagnostic: \\\\\\\"Your Marketing score suggests lead generation is a gap. Fix that first.\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n### Pricing Optimization Modeler:\\\\n- Current pricing \\\\u2192 revenue at current volume\\\\n- \\\\\\\"What if you raised prices 10%?\\\\\\\" \\\\u2192 projected revenue assuming X% volume loss\\\\n- Industry benchmarks for price sensitivity\\\\n- Connected to Marketing & Sales diagnostic: \\\\\\\"Your value proposition score is 2/5 \\\\u2014 strengthen that before raising prices\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n### Revenue Scenario Planner:\\\\n- \\\\\\\"Model: What does $500K revenue look like?\\\\\\\"\\\\n- Sliders: Price per unit, units per month, # customers, retention rate\\\\n- Output: \\\\\\\"To hit $500K, you need 42 customers paying $1,000/mo with 85% annual retention\\\\\\\"\\\\n- \\\\\\\"Your current trajectory puts you at $500K in 18 months\\\\\\\" (or identifies the gap)\\\\n\\\\n---\\\\n\\\\n## 14. LIFECYCLE REVENUE FEATURES\\\\n\\\\nNot just getting coaches and clients on \\\\u2014 maximizing revenue per relationship over time:\\\\n\\\\n### Automated Re-Engagement:\\\\n- Client goes inactive \\\\u2192 30 days later: \\\\\\\"It's been a month. Here's your year-in-review.\\\\\\\" (auto-generated from data)\\\\n\\\\n### Upsell Paths (Built Into the Product):\\\\n| Current State | Trigger | Upsell |\\\\n|--------------|---------|--------|\\\\n| 1:1 coaching only | Client mentions team issues | \\\\\\\"Consider adding Team Health Pulse survey\\\\\\\" |\\\\n| No 360\\\\u00b0 feedback | Client's blind spots visible in notes | \\\\\\\"360\\\\u00b0 feedback could validate this\\\\\\\" |\\\\n| Standard engagement | High BHS improvement | \\\\\\\"Your progress is exceptional. ONBOARDING UX \\\\\\\\u2014 THE FIRST 5 MINUTES\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nThe #1 reason SaaS products fail is onboarding. 40-60% of free trial users never come back after day 1. BoldEdge needs the best onboarding in coaching SaaS.\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Coach Onboarding Flow (First Login)\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n**Screen 1: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"What brings you here?\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"**\\\\\\\\n- [ ] I'm an executive/business coach\\\\\\\\n- [ ] I run peer groups or masterminds\\\\\\\\n- [ ] I'm exploring coaching tools\\\\\\\\n- [ ] My colleague referred me\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\u2192 Routes to personalized setup based on answer\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n**Screen 2: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Let's set up your practice\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" (60-second form)**\\\\\\\\n- Your name + photo\\\\\\\\n- Coaching specialty (executive, business, leadership, team, career)\\\\\\\\n- Number of active clients (0-5, 6-15, 16-30, 31+)\\\\\\\\n- Primary service (1:1, group, peer advisory, programs, mix)\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\u2192 Platform pre-configures dashboard, templates, and workflows based on answers\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n**Screen 3: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Add your first client\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" (the activation moment)**\\\\\\\\n- Simple form: Client name, email, company name\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Send them a diagnostic?\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" toggle (default ON)\\\\\\\\n- Client receives branded email invitation\\\\\\\\n- Coach sees: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Diagnostic sent! You'll see results on your dashboard when they complete it.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\u2192 **This is the \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"aha moment.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"** Coach sees the system working in real-time.\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n**Screen 4: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"While you wait, take your own diagnostic\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"**\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"See how BoldEdge works from the client's perspective\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- Coach takes the Business Health Check on their own coaching business\\\\\\\\n- Results appear in a demo dashboard\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"This is what your client will see. Now imagine 10 clients, all tracked here.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n**Screen 5: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"You're live. Here's your next 3 steps.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"**\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\u2705 Client invited (done)\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\u2b1c Schedule your first session (calendar link or manual)\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\u2b1c Customize your dashboard (pick which KPIs to track)\\\\\\\\n- Progress bar: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"You're 40% set up\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Client Onboarding Flow (First Login)\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n**Step 1:** Branded email from coach: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Welcome to BoldEdge \\\\\\\\u2014 [Coach Name] has invited you\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n**Step 2:** Click \\\\\\\\u2192 lands on diagnostic (no login required for first diagnostic)\\\\\\\\n**Step 3:** Complete diagnostic (48 questions, ~10 minutes, progress bar, save & resume)\\\\\\\\n**Step 4:** Instant results page: Business Health Score + radar chart + top 3 recommendations\\\\\\\\n**Step 5:** \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Create your account to save results and access your dashboard\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n**Step 6:** Client dashboard with pre-populated data from diagnostic\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n**Key principle:** Client gets VALUE before they create an account. The diagnostic is the product demo.\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n---\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n## 3. Here's how.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- Auto-generated from platform data (with coach approval)\\\\\\\\n- Published on BoldEdge blog + coach's profile\\\\\\\\n- SEO + social proof + lead generation for the coach\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Knowledge Base Within the Product:\\\\\\\\nNot a separate wiki \\\\\\\\u2014 embedded contextually:\\\\\\\\n- Mouse over \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Operations\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" on any diagnostic \\\\\\\\u2192 tooltip: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Operations measures your business processes, technology, delivery quality, and efficiency. MOBILE EXPERIENCE\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Coach Mobile App (Priorities):\\\\\\\\n1. **Session prep brief** \\\\\\\\u2014 swipe through client summary before a session (even in the elevator)\\\\\\\\n2. **Quick note capture** \\\\\\\\u2014 voice-to-text session notes, auto-tagged to client\\\\\\\\n3. **Task quick-add** \\\\\\\\u2014 assign a task to a client in 3 taps\\\\\\\\n4. **Notification center** \\\\\\\\u2014 overdue tasks, upcoming sessions, at-risk alerts\\\\\\\\n5. **Client quick-view** \\\\\\\\u2014 tap client name \\\\\\\\u2192 BHS score, last session, open tasks\\\\\\\\n6. **Offline mode** \\\\\\\\u2014 session notes save locally, sync when connected\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Client Mobile App (Priorities):\\\\\\\\n1. **Business Health Score** \\\\\\\\u2014 big number, front and center, swipe for trend\\\\\\\\n2. **Weekly pulse check-in** \\\\\\\\u2014 push notification Monday 8 AM, complete in 30 seconds\\\\\\\\n3. **Task list** \\\\\\\\u2014 check off completed tasks, see what's due\\\\\\\\n4. **Session reminder** \\\\\\\\u2014 with pre-session self-assessment inline\\\\\\\\n5. **Win journal** \\\\\\\\u2014 quick capture, photo attachment, voice note\\\\\\\\n6. **Habit tracker** \\\\\\\\u2014 daily check-in with streak counter\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Mobile-Specific Features:\\\\\\\\n- **Apple Watch complication** \\\\\\\\u2014 Business Health Score on watch face\\\\\\\\n- **Widget** \\\\\\\\u2014 iOS/Android home screen widget showing BHS + next session\\\\\\\\n- **Push notifications** \\\\\\\\u2014 smart timing based on user activity patterns\\\\\\\\n- **Siri/Google shortcut** \\\\\\\\u2014 \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Hey Siri, add a win to BoldEdge\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" \\\\\\\\u2192 voice capture\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n---\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n## 6. TRUST, COMPLIANCE & CREDIBILITY SIGNALS\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### ICF Alignment:\\\\\\\\n- Diagnostic questions aligned with ICF Core Competencies\\\\\\\\n- ROI calculation follows ICF-recommended methodology\\\\\\\\n- Platform displays: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"BoldEdge follows ICF measurement guidelines\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- Future: Apply for ICF Continuing Coach Education (CCE) credits for BoldEdge training\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Security & Privacy:\\\\\\\\n- SOC 2 Type II compliance roadmap (not needed at launch, but signal intent)\\\\\\\\n- End-to-end encryption for session notes and client data\\\\\\\\n- Client data owned by coach \\\\\\\\u2014 if they leave, they export everything\\\\\\\\n- Data processing agreement (DPA) available for enterprise\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Your data is never shared. Period.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" prominently on settings page\\\\\\\\n- Two-factor authentication\\\\\\\\n- Session recording stored encrypted with auto-deletion options\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Trust Signals on Every Page:\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Last backup: 2 minutes ago\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" in footer\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Data encrypted at rest and in transit\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" in security settings\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Served from [region] \\\\\\\\u2014 GDPR compliant\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" for international coaches\\\\\\\\n- Client-facing: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Your responses are confidential and shared only with your coach\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Professional Credibility:\\\\\\\\n- Coach profile: display certifications (ICF ACC/PCC/MCC, DISC, EQ-i, StrengthsFinder)\\\\\\\\n- Verified badge: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"BoldEdge Verified Coach\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" (completed training + has active clients)\\\\\\\\n- Testimonial display: verified outcome badges from the platform\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Member since [date]\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" on profile\\\\\\\\n- Aggregate stats: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Has coached 23 clients on BoldEdge with average 19-point BHS improvement\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n---\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n## 7. API & ECOSYSTEM PLAY\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Public API (Business+ tier):\\\\\\\\n- RESTful API with full documentation\\\\\\\\n- Endpoints: clients, diagnostics, sessions, tasks, KPIs, reports\\\\\\\\n- Webhook events: diagnostic_completed, session_completed, task_completed, score_changed\\\\\\\\n- Use cases:\\\\\\\\n  - Custom dashboards in client's existing tools\\\\\\\\n  - Automated data pulls into BI platforms (Tableau, Looker, PowerBI)\\\\\\\\n  - CRM sync (push BoldEdge data to Salesforce/HubSpot)\\\\\\\\n  - Custom integrations coaches build themselves\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Embeddable Widgets:\\\\\\\\n- **Diagnostic embed** \\\\\\\\u2014 coach puts diagnostic on their own website. Client completes it \\\\\\\\u2192 data flows to BoldEdge.\\\\\\\\n  - `<iframe src=\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"boldedge.io/embed/diagnostic/[coach-id]\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" />`\\\\\\\\n  - Branded with coach's logo and colors\\\\\\\\n  - This is a massive lead-gen tool. Every coach website becomes a BoldEdge acquisition channel.\\\\\\\\n- **Business Health Score badge** \\\\\\\\u2014 client embeds their score on their own site\\\\\\\\n  - Like a \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"BBB Accredited\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" badge but for business health\\\\\\\\n  - \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Business Health Score: 78 | Verified by BoldEdge\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n  - Click \\\\\\\\u2192 public profile with score trend (if client opts in)\\\\\\\\n  - Viral loop: other businesses see it and want their own score\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Zapier/Make Integration:\\\\\\\\n- Triggers: New client, Diagnostic completed, Session completed, Task overdue, Score changed, Recommendation accepted\\\\\\\\n- Actions: Create client, Add KPI data point, Create task, Send notification\\\\\\\\n- Use cases:\\\\\\\\n  - \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"When diagnostic is completed \\\\\\\\u2192 send Slack notification to coach\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n  - \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"When task is overdue by 3 days \\\\\\\\u2192 send reminder via Twilio SMS\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n  - \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"When new client added \\\\\\\\u2192 create Google Drive folder\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n  - \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"When session completed \\\\\\\\u2192 log to Airtable tracking sheet\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n---\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n## 8. ADVANCED GAMIFICATION\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nBeyond badges \\\\\\\\u2014 gamification that drives real behavioral change:\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### For Clients:\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n**Levels System:**\\\\\\\\n- Level 1: Newcomer (completed intake)\\\\\\\\n- Level 2: Committed (completed first diagnostic)\\\\\\\\n- Level 3: In Motion (completed 3 sessions + 5 tasks)\\\\\\\\n- Level 4: Gaining Traction (BHS improved 10+ points)\\\\\\\\n- Level 5: Transforming (BHS improved 20+ points)\\\\\\\\n- Level 6: High Performer (BHS above 75)\\\\\\\\n- Level 7: Elite (BHS above 85 for 3+ months)\\\\\\\\n- Level 8: Champion (BHS above 90 + refers another client)\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nEach level unlocks:\\\\\\\\n- A visual badge on their dashboard\\\\\\\\n- A shareable achievement graphic\\\\\\\\n- Access to progressively advanced resources\\\\\\\\n- At Level 5+: invitation to join a peer group of similar-level clients\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n**Streaks:**\\\\\\\\n- Weekly pulse check-in streak: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"12 weeks in a row! \\\\\\\\ud83d\\\\\\\\udd25\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- Task completion streak: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Perfect month \\\\\\\\u2014 all tasks completed on time!\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- Session attendance streak: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"6 consecutive sessions attended\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- Broken streaks trigger gentle re-engagement: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"You missed your check-in this week. Take 30 seconds to get back on track.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n**Challenges:**\\\\\\\\n- Coach assigns a 30-day challenge: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Delegate one decision per day for 30 days\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- Client checks in daily\\\\\\\\n- Completion = badge + highlight in next session prep\\\\\\\\n- Peer group challenges: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Which group member can improve their Financial score the most in 90 days?\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### For Coaches:\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n**Coaching Milestones:**\\\\\\\\n- 10th client onboarded\\\\\\\\n- 100th session completed\\\\\\\\n- 50th diagnostic run\\\\\\\\n- First client reaches BHS 80+\\\\\\\\n- 5 clients with positive ROI verified\\\\\\\\n- 1,000 coaching hours logged\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n**Leaderboard (opt-in, anonymized):**\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Your average client BHS improvement: 18 points (top 25% of BoldEdge coaches)\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Your client retention rate: 88% (top 15%)\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Your task completion rate: 85% (above average)\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- Not competitive \\\\\\\\u2014 more like \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"here's where you stand\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" benchmarking\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n---\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n## 9. WHITE-LABEL DEEP MECHANICS\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nFor Enterprise tier ($499-999/mo) \\\\\\\\u2014 what makes white-label actually valuable:\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Visual Customization:\\\\\\\\n- Custom logo (replaces BoldEdge logo everywhere)\\\\\\\\n- Custom color scheme (primary, secondary, accent)\\\\\\\\n- Custom fonts\\\\\\\\n- Custom email templates (header, footer, colors)\\\\\\\\n- Custom domain: `coaching.mereditheicher.com`\\\\\\\\n- Custom favicon\\\\\\\\n- Removable \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Powered by BoldEdge\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" footer\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Content Customization:\\\\\\\\n- Custom diagnostic questions (add, remove, reword)\\\\\\\\n- Custom scoring weights\\\\\\\\n- Custom recommendation library\\\\\\\\n- Custom templates (replace defaults with coach's own)\\\\\\\\n- Custom onboarding flow text\\\\\\\\n- Custom email copy\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Branding Deliverables:\\\\\\\\n- All PDF exports use coach's branding\\\\\\\\n- Client portal shows coach's branding only\\\\\\\\n- Proposals and agreements branded\\\\\\\\n- Diagnostic emails branded\\\\\\\\n- ROI reports branded\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### What BoldEdge Still Powers (Under the Hood):\\\\\\\\n- All infrastructure and hosting\\\\\\\\n- AI intelligence and benchmarking\\\\\\\\n- Workflow automation engine\\\\\\\\n- Data storage and security\\\\\\\\n- Platform updates and new features\\\\\\\\n- Support and maintenance\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n**Pitch to coaches:** \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Your brand. Our brain. Give your clients a world-class technology experience with your name on it.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n---\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n## 10. COACH SUCCESS SYSTEM (Reduce Coach Churn)\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nThe biggest risk to BoldEdge is coach churn. If coaches leave, everything stops. Build systems to make coaches successful:\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Coach Onboarding Checklist (First 30 Days):\\\\\\\\n- [ ] Week 1: Complete profile, take personal diagnostic, add first client\\\\\\\\n- [ ] Week 2: Client completes diagnostic, coach reviews results, first session prep used\\\\\\\\n- [ ] Week 3: First post-session follow-up workflow runs, 2+ tasks assigned\\\\\\\\n- [ ] Week 4: Second client added OR second session with first client\\\\\\\\n- Progress tracked on \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Coach Success Dashboard\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" (internal admin view)\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Automated Coach Health Monitoring:\\\\\\\\n- **Green:** Coach has 3+ active clients, runs sessions weekly, uses diagnostic\\\\\\\\n- **Yellow:** Coach has 1-2 clients, sessions sporadic, some features unused\\\\\\\\n- **Red:** Coach hasn't logged in 14+ days, no sessions in 30 days\\\\\\\\n- Automated nudges for Yellow/Red:\\\\\\\\n  - \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"You haven't run a diagnostic in 3 weeks. Want to send one to [client name]?\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n  - \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Most BoldEdge coaches see retention improve after using session prep briefs. Try it before your next session.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Coach Community:\\\\\\\\n- Private forum/Slack for BoldEdge coaches\\\\\\\\n- Monthly webinar: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"BoldEdge Best Practices\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" featuring a power user\\\\\\\\n- Template sharing: coaches contribute templates to a shared library\\\\\\\\n- Peer matching: connect coaches in non-competing markets for knowledge exchange\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Coach of the Month\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" spotlight\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Coach Training Resources:\\\\\\\\n- Video library: How to use every feature (2-3 min each)\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"The BoldEdge Method\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" \\\\\\\\u2014 a coaching methodology built around the diagnostic\\\\\\\\n  - Step 1: Diagnostic \\\\\\\\u2192 Baseline\\\\\\\\n  - Step 2: Priority Gap Identification \\\\\\\\u2192 Focus Areas\\\\\\\\n  - Step 3: Coaching Plan \\\\\\\\u2192 90-Day Objectives\\\\\\\\n  - Step 4: Session Cadence \\\\\\\\u2192 Weekly/Bi-weekly\\\\\\\\n  - Step 5: Task & Action Loop \\\\\\\\u2192 Between-Session Accountability\\\\\\\\n  - Step 6: Re-Diagnostic \\\\\\\\u2192 Measure Progress\\\\\\\\n  - Step 7: ROI Report \\\\\\\\u2192 Prove Value\\\\\\\\n  - Step 8: Renewal \\\\\\\\u2192 Continue Engagement\\\\\\\\n- Certification program (as described in Doc 06)\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n---\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n## 11. EVENT & OFFLINE INTEGRATION\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nCoaching doesn't only happen online. BoldEdge should bridge digital and in-person:\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Conference/Workshop Mode:\\\\\\\\n- Coach runs a workshop at a conference\\\\\\\\n- Audience scans QR code \\\\\\\\u2192 takes mini-diagnostic (10 questions) on phone\\\\\\\\n- Instant results on their phone\\\\\\\\n- Coach shows aggregate audience results on screen: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"This room's biggest gap is Strategy\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- CTA: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Want the full diagnostic? Connect with [coach name]\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- Every attendee becomes a lead in BoldEdge CRM\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Retreat Integration (expanded from Doc 06):\\\\\\\\n- **Pre-retreat survey** \\\\\\\\u2014 team members complete individual diagnostics\\\\\\\\n- **Live session tools:**\\\\\\\\n  - Polling: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"What's your team's #1 challenge?\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" (live results on screen)\\\\\\\\n  - Breakout group assignment based on diagnostic profiles\\\\\\\\n  - Commitment capture: each person enters their 90-day commitment on phone\\\\\\\\n- **Post-retreat:**\\\\\\\\n  - All commitments tracked as tasks in BoldEdge\\\\\\\\n  - 30/60/90 day automated check-ins\\\\\\\\n  - \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Retreat Impact Report\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" generated at 90 days\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Speaking Engagement Lead Capture:\\\\\\\\n- Coach gives a talk \\\\\\\\u2192 references Business Health Score\\\\\\\\n- Audience texts keyword to number \\\\\\\\u2192 gets diagnostic link\\\\\\\\n- Or scans QR \\\\\\\\u2192 same flow\\\\\\\\n- All leads tracked in BoldEdge with source tag: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Event: [Name] [Date]\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n---\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n## 12. AI CAPABILITIES \\\\\\\\u2014 NEXT LEVEL\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nBeyond what's in Docs 05-07:\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Voice Notes \\\\\\\\u2192 Structured Data:\\\\\\\\n- Coach records voice note after session on phone\\\\\\\\n- AI transcribes \\\\\\\\u2192 extracts: key themes, action items, mood assessment, follow-up questions\\\\\\\\n- Auto-files to correct client's session record\\\\\\\\n- Coach reviews and approves \\\\\\\\u2192 action items auto-created as tasks\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### AI Coaching Debrief Partner:\\\\\\\\n- After a session, coach can \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"debrief\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" with AI:\\\\\\\\n  - \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"The session with James went off track. He kept avoiding the financial review discussion.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n  - AI responds: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"This is the 3rd time James has avoided financials. His Financial diagnostic score is 38. Consider opening next session with: 'James, I've noticed a pattern...'\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- Not replacing coaching supervision \\\\\\\\u2014 augmenting it with data-backed reflection\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Predictive Client Matching:\\\\\\\\n- When a new prospect enters (via diagnostic or referral), AI suggests:\\\\\\\\n  - \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"This prospect profiles similarly to your most successful clients (Sarah, Mark). Recommend 1:1 Premium engagement.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n  - \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"This prospect's profile matches clients who churn within 3 months at the Starter level. Consider a deeper onboarding.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Natural Language KPI Entry:\\\\\\\\n- Client types: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Revenue was $142K this month, up from $128K last month. Hired 2 new people.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- AI parses \\\\\\\\u2192 updates KPIs: Revenue: $142,000 (+10.9%), Headcount: +2\\\\\\\\n- Coach sees updated dashboard without manual data entry\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### AI-Generated Quarterly Business Review:\\\\\\\\n- Every 90 days, AI auto-generates a comprehensive review:\\\\\\\\n  - BHS trend\\\\\\\\n  - KPI changes\\\\\\\\n  - Tasks completed and impact\\\\\\\\n  - Diagnostic score changes\\\\\\\\n  - Sessions attended\\\\\\\\n  - Wins logged\\\\\\\\n  - Recommendations accepted and status\\\\\\\\n- Coach reviews, edits, and sends to client as a branded PDF\\\\\\\\n- Saves 2-3 hours per client per quarter\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n---\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n## 13. FINANCIAL MODELING SUITE (For Business-Focused Coaching)\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nBeyond the cash runway calculator \\\\\\\\u2014 a light financial modeling layer:\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Break-Even Calculator:\\\\\\\\n- Inputs: Fixed costs, variable costs, average revenue per unit\\\\\\\\n- Output: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"You need 47 customers to break even. You currently have 38.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- Scenario: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"If you raise prices 15%, breakeven drops to 41 customers\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Hiring ROI Calculator:\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"If you hire a salesperson at $65K + $20K commission potential...\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"They need to generate $127K in revenue to be ROI-positive\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Based on your current close rate of 22%, they need 48 leads/month\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- Connected to diagnostic: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Your Marketing score suggests lead generation is a gap. Fix that first.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Pricing Optimization Modeler:\\\\\\\\n- Current pricing \\\\\\\\u2192 revenue at current volume\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"What if you raised prices 10%?\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" \\\\\\\\u2192 projected revenue assuming X% volume loss\\\\\\\\n- Industry benchmarks for price sensitivity\\\\\\\\n- Connected to Marketing & Sales diagnostic: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Your value proposition score is 2/5 \\\\\\\\u2014 strengthen that before raising prices\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Revenue Scenario Planner:\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Model: What does $500K revenue look like?\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- Sliders: Price per unit, units per month, # customers, retention rate\\\\\\\\n- Output: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"To hit $500K, you need 42 customers paying $1,000/mo with 85% annual retention\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Your current trajectory puts you at $500K in 18 months\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" (or identifies the gap)\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n---\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n## 14. LIFECYCLE REVENUE FEATURES\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nNot just getting coaches and clients on \\\\\\\\u2014 maximizing revenue per relationship over time:\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Automated Re-Engagement:\\\\\\\\n- Client goes inactive \\\\\\\\u2192 30 days later: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"It's been a month. Your Business Health Score was 67 \\\\\\\\u2014 want to check in?\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- 90 days: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"3 months since your last session. Companies that maintain coaching see 2.4x better outcomes.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- 6 months: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Time for your semi-annual diagnostic? See how your business has evolved.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- Annual: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"It's your coaching anniversary! Here's your year-in-review.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" (auto-generated from data)\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Upsell Paths (Built Into the Product):\\\\\\\\n| Current State | Trigger | Upsell |\\\\\\\\n|--------------|---------|--------|\\\\\\\\n| 1:1 coaching only | Client mentions team issues | \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Consider adding Team Health Pulse survey\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" |\\\\\\\\n| No 360\\\\\\\\u00b0 feedback | Client's blind spots visible in notes | \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"360\\\\\\\\u00b0 feedback could validate this\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" |\\\\\\\\n| Standard engagement | High BHS improvement | \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Your progress is exceptional. Consider a Quarterly Business Review package\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" |\\\\\\\\n| Individual client | Client is CEO of 50+ person company | \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Your leadership team could benefit from group diagnostics\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" |\\\\\\\\n| Coaching only | Client mentions board meeting | \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"BoldEdge can generate a Board Prep Kit\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" |\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Package Bundling:\\\\\\\\n- Coach creates custom bundles: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Growth Accelerator\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" = 12 sessions + 2 diagnostics + 1 retreat + monthly KPI review\\\\\\\\n- Platform tracks usage against package\\\\\\\\n- Auto-alerts: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Client has 2 sessions remaining in their package. Renewal conversation recommended.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- Suggested pricing based on component value: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"This bundle's component value is $8,500. Recommend bundle price: $6,500-$7,500.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Seasonal Campaigns:\\\\\\\\n- **January:** \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"New Year Business Health Check\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" \\\\\\\\u2014 free diagnostic promotion for coaches to offer prospects\\\\\\\\n- **Q1:** \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Q1 Planning Workshop\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" \\\\\\\\u2014 coaches get a turnkey workshop kit (slides, workbook, diagnostic link)\\\\\\\\n- **Mid-Year:** \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Mid-Year Business Review\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" \\\\\\\\u2014 re-diagnostic push for all active clients\\\\\\\\n- **Q4:** \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Year in Review\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" \\\\\\\\u2014 auto-generated annual summary for every client\\\\\\\\n- Each campaign comes with: email templates, social media copy, workshop materials, diagnostic links\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n---\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n## 15. In funnels, once a user reaches the end, the process resets. This led to inefficiencies, especially in digital products, where growth isn\\\\u2019t just about a one-time sale but rather a matter of user engagement. The term \\\\\\\"growth loop\\\\\\\" gained traction around 2016 when growth-focused teams at tech companies began to look for ways to optimize user acquisition and retention through viral effects and product strategies. These companies realized that product-led growth could be driven internally by user behavior instead of relying solely on external marketing campaigns. The loop concept allowed for more efficient, scalable growth. For example, a user signs up for a service, finds value in it, and then invites others to join.\\\\n\\\\nThe beauty of growth loops is their compounding effect\\\\u2014they build on themselves. ADVANCED GAMIFICATION\\\\n\\\\nBeyond badges \\\\u2014 gamification that drives real behavioral change:\\\\n\\\\n### For Clients:\\\\n\\\\n**Levels System:**\\\\n- Level 1: Newcomer (completed intake)\\\\n- Level 2: Committed (completed first diagnostic)\\\\n- Level 3: In Motion (completed 3 sessions + 5 tasks)\\\\n- Level 4: Gaining Traction (BHS improved 10+ points)\\\\n- Level 5: Transforming (BHS improved 20+ points)\\\\n- Level 6: High Performer (BHS above 75)\\\\n- Level 7: Elite (BHS above 85 for 3+ months)\\\\n- Level 8: Champion (BHS above 90 + refers another client)\\\\n\\\\nEach level unlocks:\\\\n- A visual badge on their dashboard\\\\n- A shareable achievement graphic\\\\n- Access to progressively advanced resources\\\\n- At Level 5+: invitation to join a peer group of similar-level clients\\\\n\\\\n**Streaks:**\\\\n- Weekly pulse check-in streak: \\\\\\\"12 weeks in a row! \\\\ud83d\\\\udd25\\\\\\\"\\\\n- Task completion streak: \\\\\\\"Perfect month \\\\u2014 all tasks completed on time!\\\\\\\"\\\\n- Session attendance streak: \\\\\\\"6 consecutive sessions attended\\\\\\\"\\\\n- Broken streaks trigger gentle re-engagement: \\\\\\\"You missed your check-in this week. Take 30 seconds to get back on track.\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n**Challenges:**\\\\n- Coach assigns a 30-day challenge: \\\\\\\"Delegate one decision per day for 30 days\\\\\\\"\\\\n- Client checks in daily\\\\n- Completion = badge + highlight in next session prep\\\\n- Peer group challenges: \\\\\\\"Which group member can improve their Financial score the most in 90 days?\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n### For Coaches:\\\\n\\\\n**Coaching Milestones:**\\\\n- 10th client onboarded\\\\n- 100th session completed\\\\n- 50th diagnostic run\\\\n- First client reaches BHS 80+\\\\n- 5 clients with positive ROI verified\\\\n- 1,000 coaching hours logged\\\\n\\\\n**Leaderboard (opt-in, anonymized):**\\\\n- \\\\\\\"Your average client BHS improvement: 18 points (top 25% of BoldEdge coaches)\\\\\\\"\\\\n- \\\\\\\"Your client retention rate: 88% (top 15%)\\\\\\\"\\\\n- \\\\\\\"Your task completion rate: 85% (above average)\\\\\\\"\\\\n- Not competitive \\\\u2014 more like \\\\\\\"here's where you stand\\\\\\\" benchmarking\\\\n\\\\n---\\\\n\\\\n## 9. FINANCIAL MODELING SUITE (For Business-Focused Coaching)\\\\n\\\\nBeyond the cash runway calculator \\\\u2014 a light financial modeling layer:\\\\n\\\\n### Break-Even Calculator:\\\\n- Inputs: Fixed costs, variable costs, average revenue per unit\\\\n- Output: \\\\\\\"You need 47 customers to break even. You currently have 38.\\\\\\\"\\\\n- Scenario: \\\\\\\"If you raise prices 15%, breakeven drops to 41 customers\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n### Hiring ROI Calculator:\\\\n- \\\\\\\"If you hire a salesperson at $65K + $20K commission potential...\\\\\\\"\\\\n- \\\\\\\"They need to generate $127K in revenue to be ROI-positive\\\\\\\"\\\\n- \\\\\\\"Based on your current close rate of 22%, they need 48 leads/month\\\\\\\"\\\\n- Connected to diagnostic: \\\\\\\"Your Marketing score suggests lead generation is a gap. Fix that first.\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n### Pricing Optimization Modeler:\\\\n- Current pricing \\\\u2192 revenue at current volume\\\\n- \\\\\\\"What if you raised prices 10%?\\\\\\\" \\\\u2192 projected revenue assuming X% volume loss\\\\n- Industry benchmarks for price sensitivity\\\\n- Connected to Marketing & Sales diagnostic: \\\\\\\"Your value proposition score is 2/5 \\\\u2014 strengthen that before raising prices\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n### Revenue Scenario Planner:\\\\n- \\\\\\\"Model: What does $500K revenue look like?\\\\\\\"\\\\n- Sliders: Price per unit, units per month, # customers, retention rate\\\\n- Output: \\\\\\\"To hit $500K, you need 42 customers paying $1,000/mo with 85% annual retention\\\\\\\"\\\\n- \\\\\\\"Your current trajectory puts you at $500K in 18 months\\\\\\\" (or identifies the gap)\\\\n\\\\n---\\\\n\\\\n## 14. LIFECYCLE REVENUE FEATURES\\\\n\\\\nNot just getting coaches and clients on \\\\u2014 maximizing revenue per relationship over time:\\\\n\\\\n### Automated Re-Engagement:\\\\n- Client goes inactive \\\\u2192 30 days later: \\\\\\\"It's been a month. Here's your year-in-review.\\\\\\\" (auto-generated from data)\\\\n\\\\n### Upsell Paths (Built Into the Product):\\\\n| Current State | Trigger | Upsell |\\\\n|--------------|---------|--------|\\\\n| 1:1 coaching only | Client mentions team issues | \\\\\\\"Consider adding Team Health Pulse survey\\\\\\\" |\\\\n| No 360\\\\u00b0 feedback | Client's blind spots visible in notes | \\\\\\\"360\\\\u00b0 feedback could validate this\\\\\\\" |\\\\n| Standard engagement | High BHS improvement | \\\\\\\"Your progress is exceptional. ONBOARDING UX \\\\\\\\u2014 THE FIRST 5 MINUTES\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nThe #1 reason SaaS products fail is onboarding. 40-60% of free trial users never come back after day 1. BoldEdge needs the best onboarding in coaching SaaS.\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Coach Onboarding Flow (First Login)\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n**Screen 1: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"What brings you here?\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"**\\\\\\\\n- [ ] I'm an executive/business coach\\\\\\\\n- [ ] I run peer groups or masterminds\\\\\\\\n- [ ] I'm exploring coaching tools\\\\\\\\n- [ ] My colleague referred me\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\u2192 Routes to personalized setup based on answer\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n**Screen 2: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Let's set up your practice\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" (60-second form)**\\\\\\\\n- Your name + photo\\\\\\\\n- Coaching specialty (executive, business, leadership, team, career)\\\\\\\\n- Number of active clients (0-5, 6-15, 16-30, 31+)\\\\\\\\n- Primary service (1:1, group, peer advisory, programs, mix)\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\u2192 Platform pre-configures dashboard, templates, and workflows based on answers\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n**Screen 3: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Add your first client\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" (the activation moment)**\\\\\\\\n- Simple form: Client name, email, company name\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Send them a diagnostic?\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" toggle (default ON)\\\\\\\\n- Client receives branded email invitation\\\\\\\\n- Coach sees: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Diagnostic sent! You'll see results on your dashboard when they complete it.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\u2192 **This is the \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"aha moment.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"** Coach sees the system working in real-time.\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n**Screen 4: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"While you wait, take your own diagnostic\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"**\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"See how BoldEdge works from the client's perspective\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- Coach takes the Business Health Check on their own coaching business\\\\\\\\n- Results appear in a demo dashboard\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"This is what your client will see. Now imagine 10 clients, all tracked here.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n**Screen 5: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"You're live. Here's your next 3 steps.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"**\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\u2705 Client invited (done)\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\u2b1c Schedule your first session (calendar link or manual)\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\u2b1c Customize your dashboard (pick which KPIs to track)\\\\\\\\n- Progress bar: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"You're 40% set up\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Client Onboarding Flow (First Login)\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n**Step 1:** Branded email from coach: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Welcome to BoldEdge \\\\\\\\u2014 [Coach Name] has invited you\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n**Step 2:** Click \\\\\\\\u2192 lands on diagnostic (no login required for first diagnostic)\\\\\\\\n**Step 3:** Complete diagnostic (48 questions, ~10 minutes, progress bar, save & resume)\\\\\\\\n**Step 4:** Instant results page: Business Health Score + radar chart + top 3 recommendations\\\\\\\\n**Step 5:** \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Create your account to save results and access your dashboard\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n**Step 6:** Client dashboard with pre-populated data from diagnostic\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n**Key principle:** Client gets VALUE before they create an account. The diagnostic is the product demo.\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n---\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n## 3. Here's how.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- Auto-generated from platform data (with coach approval)\\\\\\\\n- Published on BoldEdge blog + coach's profile\\\\\\\\n- SEO + social proof + lead generation for the coach\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Knowledge Base Within the Product:\\\\\\\\nNot a separate wiki \\\\\\\\u2014 embedded contextually:\\\\\\\\n- Mouse over \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Operations\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" on any diagnostic \\\\\\\\u2192 tooltip: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Operations measures your business processes, technology, delivery quality, and efficiency. MOBILE EXPERIENCE\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Coach Mobile App (Priorities):\\\\\\\\n1. **Session prep brief** \\\\\\\\u2014 swipe through client summary before a session (even in the elevator)\\\\\\\\n2. **Quick note capture** \\\\\\\\u2014 voice-to-text session notes, auto-tagged to client\\\\\\\\n3. **Task quick-add** \\\\\\\\u2014 assign a task to a client in 3 taps\\\\\\\\n4. **Notification center** \\\\\\\\u2014 overdue tasks, upcoming sessions, at-risk alerts\\\\\\\\n5. **Client quick-view** \\\\\\\\u2014 tap client name \\\\\\\\u2192 BHS score, last session, open tasks\\\\\\\\n6. **Offline mode** \\\\\\\\u2014 session notes save locally, sync when connected\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Client Mobile App (Priorities):\\\\\\\\n1. **Business Health Score** \\\\\\\\u2014 big number, front and center, swipe for trend\\\\\\\\n2. **Weekly pulse check-in** \\\\\\\\u2014 push notification Monday 8 AM, complete in 30 seconds\\\\\\\\n3. **Task list** \\\\\\\\u2014 check off completed tasks, see what's due\\\\\\\\n4. **Session reminder** \\\\\\\\u2014 with pre-session self-assessment inline\\\\\\\\n5. **Win journal** \\\\\\\\u2014 quick capture, photo attachment, voice note\\\\\\\\n6. **Habit tracker** \\\\\\\\u2014 daily check-in with streak counter\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Mobile-Specific Features:\\\\\\\\n- **Apple Watch complication** \\\\\\\\u2014 Business Health Score on watch face\\\\\\\\n- **Widget** \\\\\\\\u2014 iOS/Android home screen widget showing BHS + next session\\\\\\\\n- **Push notifications** \\\\\\\\u2014 smart timing based on user activity patterns\\\\\\\\n- **Siri/Google shortcut** \\\\\\\\u2014 \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Hey Siri, add a win to BoldEdge\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" \\\\\\\\u2192 voice capture\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n---\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n## 6. TRUST, COMPLIANCE & CREDIBILITY SIGNALS\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### ICF Alignment:\\\\\\\\n- Diagnostic questions aligned with ICF Core Competencies\\\\\\\\n- ROI calculation follows ICF-recommended methodology\\\\\\\\n- Platform displays: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"BoldEdge follows ICF measurement guidelines\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- Future: Apply for ICF Continuing Coach Education (CCE) credits for BoldEdge training\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Security & Privacy:\\\\\\\\n- SOC 2 Type II compliance roadmap (not needed at launch, but signal intent)\\\\\\\\n- End-to-end encryption for session notes and client data\\\\\\\\n- Client data owned by coach \\\\\\\\u2014 if they leave, they export everything\\\\\\\\n- Data processing agreement (DPA) available for enterprise\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Your data is never shared. Period.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" prominently on settings page\\\\\\\\n- Two-factor authentication\\\\\\\\n- Session recording stored encrypted with auto-deletion options\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Trust Signals on Every Page:\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Last backup: 2 minutes ago\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" in footer\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Data encrypted at rest and in transit\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" in security settings\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Served from [region] \\\\\\\\u2014 GDPR compliant\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" for international coaches\\\\\\\\n- Client-facing: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Your responses are confidential and shared only with your coach\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Professional Credibility:\\\\\\\\n- Coach profile: display certifications (ICF ACC/PCC/MCC, DISC, EQ-i, StrengthsFinder)\\\\\\\\n- Verified badge: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"BoldEdge Verified Coach\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" (completed training + has active clients)\\\\\\\\n- Testimonial display: verified outcome badges from the platform\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Member since [date]\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" on profile\\\\\\\\n- Aggregate stats: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Has coached 23 clients on BoldEdge with average 19-point BHS improvement\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n---\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n## 7. API & ECOSYSTEM PLAY\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Public API (Business+ tier):\\\\\\\\n- RESTful API with full documentation\\\\\\\\n- Endpoints: clients, diagnostics, sessions, tasks, KPIs, reports\\\\\\\\n- Webhook events: diagnostic_completed, session_completed, task_completed, score_changed\\\\\\\\n- Use cases:\\\\\\\\n  - Custom dashboards in client's existing tools\\\\\\\\n  - Automated data pulls into BI platforms (Tableau, Looker, PowerBI)\\\\\\\\n  - CRM sync (push BoldEdge data to Salesforce/HubSpot)\\\\\\\\n  - Custom integrations coaches build themselves\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Embeddable Widgets:\\\\\\\\n- **Diagnostic embed** \\\\\\\\u2014 coach puts diagnostic on their own website. Client completes it \\\\\\\\u2192 data flows to BoldEdge.\\\\\\\\n  - `<iframe src=\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"boldedge.io/embed/diagnostic/[coach-id]\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" />`\\\\\\\\n  - Branded with coach's logo and colors\\\\\\\\n  - This is a massive lead-gen tool. Every coach website becomes a BoldEdge acquisition channel.\\\\\\\\n- **Business Health Score badge** \\\\\\\\u2014 client embeds their score on their own site\\\\\\\\n  - Like a \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"BBB Accredited\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" badge but for business health\\\\\\\\n  - \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Business Health Score: 78 | Verified by BoldEdge\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n  - Click \\\\\\\\u2192 public profile with score trend (if client opts in)\\\\\\\\n  - Viral loop: other businesses see it and want their own score\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Zapier/Make Integration:\\\\\\\\n- Triggers: New client, Diagnostic completed, Session completed, Task overdue, Score changed, Recommendation accepted\\\\\\\\n- Actions: Create client, Add KPI data point, Create task, Send notification\\\\\\\\n- Use cases:\\\\\\\\n  - \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"When diagnostic is completed \\\\\\\\u2192 send Slack notification to coach\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n  - \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"When task is overdue by 3 days \\\\\\\\u2192 send reminder via Twilio SMS\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n  - \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"When new client added \\\\\\\\u2192 create Google Drive folder\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n  - \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"When session completed \\\\\\\\u2192 log to Airtable tracking sheet\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n---\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n## 8. ADVANCED GAMIFICATION\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nBeyond badges \\\\\\\\u2014 gamification that drives real behavioral change:\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### For Clients:\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n**Levels System:**\\\\\\\\n- Level 1: Newcomer (completed intake)\\\\\\\\n- Level 2: Committed (completed first diagnostic)\\\\\\\\n- Level 3: In Motion (completed 3 sessions + 5 tasks)\\\\\\\\n- Level 4: Gaining Traction (BHS improved 10+ points)\\\\\\\\n- Level 5: Transforming (BHS improved 20+ points)\\\\\\\\n- Level 6: High Performer (BHS above 75)\\\\\\\\n- Level 7: Elite (BHS above 85 for 3+ months)\\\\\\\\n- Level 8: Champion (BHS above 90 + refers another client)\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nEach level unlocks:\\\\\\\\n- A visual badge on their dashboard\\\\\\\\n- A shareable achievement graphic\\\\\\\\n- Access to progressively advanced resources\\\\\\\\n- At Level 5+: invitation to join a peer group of similar-level clients\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n**Streaks:**\\\\\\\\n- Weekly pulse check-in streak: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"12 weeks in a row! \\\\\\\\ud83d\\\\\\\\udd25\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- Task completion streak: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Perfect month \\\\\\\\u2014 all tasks completed on time!\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- Session attendance streak: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"6 consecutive sessions attended\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- Broken streaks trigger gentle re-engagement: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"You missed your check-in this week. Take 30 seconds to get back on track.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n**Challenges:**\\\\\\\\n- Coach assigns a 30-day challenge: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Delegate one decision per day for 30 days\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- Client checks in daily\\\\\\\\n- Completion = badge + highlight in next session prep\\\\\\\\n- Peer group challenges: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Which group member can improve their Financial score the most in 90 days?\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### For Coaches:\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n**Coaching Milestones:**\\\\\\\\n- 10th client onboarded\\\\\\\\n- 100th session completed\\\\\\\\n- 50th diagnostic run\\\\\\\\n- First client reaches BHS 80+\\\\\\\\n- 5 clients with positive ROI verified\\\\\\\\n- 1,000 coaching hours logged\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n**Leaderboard (opt-in, anonymized):**\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Your average client BHS improvement: 18 points (top 25% of BoldEdge coaches)\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Your client retention rate: 88% (top 15%)\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Your task completion rate: 85% (above average)\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- Not competitive \\\\\\\\u2014 more like \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"here's where you stand\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" benchmarking\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n---\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n## 9. WHITE-LABEL DEEP MECHANICS\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nFor Enterprise tier ($499-999/mo) \\\\\\\\u2014 what makes white-label actually valuable:\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Visual Customization:\\\\\\\\n- Custom logo (replaces BoldEdge logo everywhere)\\\\\\\\n- Custom color scheme (primary, secondary, accent)\\\\\\\\n- Custom fonts\\\\\\\\n- Custom email templates (header, footer, colors)\\\\\\\\n- Custom domain: `coaching.mereditheicher.com`\\\\\\\\n- Custom favicon\\\\\\\\n- Removable \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Powered by BoldEdge\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" footer\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Content Customization:\\\\\\\\n- Custom diagnostic questions (add, remove, reword)\\\\\\\\n- Custom scoring weights\\\\\\\\n- Custom recommendation library\\\\\\\\n- Custom templates (replace defaults with coach's own)\\\\\\\\n- Custom onboarding flow text\\\\\\\\n- Custom email copy\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Branding Deliverables:\\\\\\\\n- All PDF exports use coach's branding\\\\\\\\n- Client portal shows coach's branding only\\\\\\\\n- Proposals and agreements branded\\\\\\\\n- Diagnostic emails branded\\\\\\\\n- ROI reports branded\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### What BoldEdge Still Powers (Under the Hood):\\\\\\\\n- All infrastructure and hosting\\\\\\\\n- AI intelligence and benchmarking\\\\\\\\n- Workflow automation engine\\\\\\\\n- Data storage and security\\\\\\\\n- Platform updates and new features\\\\\\\\n- Support and maintenance\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n**Pitch to coaches:** \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Your brand. Our brain. Give your clients a world-class technology experience with your name on it.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n---\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n## 10. COACH SUCCESS SYSTEM (Reduce Coach Churn)\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nThe biggest risk to BoldEdge is coach churn. If coaches leave, everything stops. Build systems to make coaches successful:\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Coach Onboarding Checklist (First 30 Days):\\\\\\\\n- [ ] Week 1: Complete profile, take personal diagnostic, add first client\\\\\\\\n- [ ] Week 2: Client completes diagnostic, coach reviews results, first session prep used\\\\\\\\n- [ ] Week 3: First post-session follow-up workflow runs, 2+ tasks assigned\\\\\\\\n- [ ] Week 4: Second client added OR second session with first client\\\\\\\\n- Progress tracked on \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Coach Success Dashboard\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" (internal admin view)\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Automated Coach Health Monitoring:\\\\\\\\n- **Green:** Coach has 3+ active clients, runs sessions weekly, uses diagnostic\\\\\\\\n- **Yellow:** Coach has 1-2 clients, sessions sporadic, some features unused\\\\\\\\n- **Red:** Coach hasn't logged in 14+ days, no sessions in 30 days\\\\\\\\n- Automated nudges for Yellow/Red:\\\\\\\\n  - \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"You haven't run a diagnostic in 3 weeks. Want to send one to [client name]?\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n  - \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Most BoldEdge coaches see retention improve after using session prep briefs. Try it before your next session.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Coach Community:\\\\\\\\n- Private forum/Slack for BoldEdge coaches\\\\\\\\n- Monthly webinar: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"BoldEdge Best Practices\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" featuring a power user\\\\\\\\n- Template sharing: coaches contribute templates to a shared library\\\\\\\\n- Peer matching: connect coaches in non-competing markets for knowledge exchange\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Coach of the Month\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" spotlight\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Coach Training Resources:\\\\\\\\n- Video library: How to use every feature (2-3 min each)\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"The BoldEdge Method\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" \\\\\\\\u2014 a coaching methodology built around the diagnostic\\\\\\\\n  - Step 1: Diagnostic \\\\\\\\u2192 Baseline\\\\\\\\n  - Step 2: Priority Gap Identification \\\\\\\\u2192 Focus Areas\\\\\\\\n  - Step 3: Coaching Plan \\\\\\\\u2192 90-Day Objectives\\\\\\\\n  - Step 4: Session Cadence \\\\\\\\u2192 Weekly/Bi-weekly\\\\\\\\n  - Step 5: Task & Action Loop \\\\\\\\u2192 Between-Session Accountability\\\\\\\\n  - Step 6: Re-Diagnostic \\\\\\\\u2192 Measure Progress\\\\\\\\n  - Step 7: ROI Report \\\\\\\\u2192 Prove Value\\\\\\\\n  - Step 8: Renewal \\\\\\\\u2192 Continue Engagement\\\\\\\\n- Certification program (as described in Doc 06)\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n---\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n## 11. EVENT & OFFLINE INTEGRATION\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nCoaching doesn't only happen online. BoldEdge should bridge digital and in-person:\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Conference/Workshop Mode:\\\\\\\\n- Coach runs a workshop at a conference\\\\\\\\n- Audience scans QR code \\\\\\\\u2192 takes mini-diagnostic (10 questions) on phone\\\\\\\\n- Instant results on their phone\\\\\\\\n- Coach shows aggregate audience results on screen: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"This room's biggest gap is Strategy\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- CTA: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Want the full diagnostic? Connect with [coach name]\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- Every attendee becomes a lead in BoldEdge CRM\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Retreat Integration (expanded from Doc 06):\\\\\\\\n- **Pre-retreat survey** \\\\\\\\u2014 team members complete individual diagnostics\\\\\\\\n- **Live session tools:**\\\\\\\\n  - Polling: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"What's your team's #1 challenge?\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" (live results on screen)\\\\\\\\n  - Breakout group assignment based on diagnostic profiles\\\\\\\\n  - Commitment capture: each person enters their 90-day commitment on phone\\\\\\\\n- **Post-retreat:**\\\\\\\\n  - All commitments tracked as tasks in BoldEdge\\\\\\\\n  - 30/60/90 day automated check-ins\\\\\\\\n  - \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Retreat Impact Report\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" generated at 90 days\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Speaking Engagement Lead Capture:\\\\\\\\n- Coach gives a talk \\\\\\\\u2192 references Business Health Score\\\\\\\\n- Audience texts keyword to number \\\\\\\\u2192 gets diagnostic link\\\\\\\\n- Or scans QR \\\\\\\\u2192 same flow\\\\\\\\n- All leads tracked in BoldEdge with source tag: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Event: [Name] [Date]\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n---\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n## 12. AI CAPABILITIES \\\\\\\\u2014 NEXT LEVEL\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nBeyond what's in Docs 05-07:\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Voice Notes \\\\\\\\u2192 Structured Data:\\\\\\\\n- Coach records voice note after session on phone\\\\\\\\n- AI transcribes \\\\\\\\u2192 extracts: key themes, action items, mood assessment, follow-up questions\\\\\\\\n- Auto-files to correct client's session record\\\\\\\\n- Coach reviews and approves \\\\\\\\u2192 action items auto-created as tasks\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### AI Coaching Debrief Partner:\\\\\\\\n- After a session, coach can \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"debrief\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" with AI:\\\\\\\\n  - \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"The session with James went off track. He kept avoiding the financial review discussion.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n  - AI responds: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"This is the 3rd time James has avoided financials. His Financial diagnostic score is 38. Consider opening next session with: 'James, I've noticed a pattern...'\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- Not replacing coaching supervision \\\\\\\\u2014 augmenting it with data-backed reflection\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Predictive Client Matching:\\\\\\\\n- When a new prospect enters (via diagnostic or referral), AI suggests:\\\\\\\\n  - \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"This prospect profiles similarly to your most successful clients (Sarah, Mark). Recommend 1:1 Premium engagement.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n  - \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"This prospect's profile matches clients who churn within 3 months at the Starter level. Consider a deeper onboarding.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Natural Language KPI Entry:\\\\\\\\n- Client types: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Revenue was $142K this month, up from $128K last month. Hired 2 new people.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- AI parses \\\\\\\\u2192 updates KPIs: Revenue: $142,000 (+10.9%), Headcount: +2\\\\\\\\n- Coach sees updated dashboard without manual data entry\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### AI-Generated Quarterly Business Review:\\\\\\\\n- Every 90 days, AI auto-generates a comprehensive review:\\\\\\\\n  - BHS trend\\\\\\\\n  - KPI changes\\\\\\\\n  - Tasks completed and impact\\\\\\\\n  - Diagnostic score changes\\\\\\\\n  - Sessions attended\\\\\\\\n  - Wins logged\\\\\\\\n  - Recommendations accepted and status\\\\\\\\n- Coach reviews, edits, and sends to client as a branded PDF\\\\\\\\n- Saves 2-3 hours per client per quarter\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n---\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n## 13. FINANCIAL MODELING SUITE (For Business-Focused Coaching)\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nBeyond the cash runway calculator \\\\\\\\u2014 a light financial modeling layer:\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Break-Even Calculator:\\\\\\\\n- Inputs: Fixed costs, variable costs, average revenue per unit\\\\\\\\n- Output: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"You need 47 customers to break even. You currently have 38.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- Scenario: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"If you raise prices 15%, breakeven drops to 41 customers\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Hiring ROI Calculator:\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"If you hire a salesperson at $65K + $20K commission potential...\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"They need to generate $127K in revenue to be ROI-positive\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Based on your current close rate of 22%, they need 48 leads/month\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- Connected to diagnostic: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Your Marketing score suggests lead generation is a gap. Fix that first.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Pricing Optimization Modeler:\\\\\\\\n- Current pricing \\\\\\\\u2192 revenue at current volume\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"What if you raised prices 10%?\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" \\\\\\\\u2192 projected revenue assuming X% volume loss\\\\\\\\n- Industry benchmarks for price sensitivity\\\\\\\\n- Connected to Marketing & Sales diagnostic: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Your value proposition score is 2/5 \\\\\\\\u2014 strengthen that before raising prices\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Revenue Scenario Planner:\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Model: What does $500K revenue look like?\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- Sliders: Price per unit, units per month, # customers, retention rate\\\\\\\\n- Output: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"To hit $500K, you need 42 customers paying $1,000/mo with 85% annual retention\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Your current trajectory puts you at $500K in 18 months\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" (or identifies the gap)\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n---\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n## 14. LIFECYCLE REVENUE FEATURES\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nNot just getting coaches and clients on \\\\\\\\u2014 maximizing revenue per relationship over time:\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Automated Re-Engagement:\\\\\\\\n- Client goes inactive \\\\\\\\u2192 30 days later: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"It's been a month. Your Business Health Score was 67 \\\\\\\\u2014 want to check in?\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- 90 days: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"3 months since your last session. Companies that maintain coaching see 2.4x better outcomes.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- 6 months: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Time for your semi-annual diagnostic? See how your business has evolved.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- Annual: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"It's your coaching anniversary! Here's your year-in-review.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" (auto-generated from data)\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Upsell Paths (Built Into the Product):\\\\\\\\n| Current State | Trigger | Upsell |\\\\\\\\n|--------------|---------|--------|\\\\\\\\n| 1:1 coaching only | Client mentions team issues | \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Consider adding Team Health Pulse survey\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" |\\\\\\\\n| No 360\\\\\\\\u00b0 feedback | Client's blind spots visible in notes | \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"360\\\\\\\\u00b0 feedback could validate this\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" |\\\\\\\\n| Standard engagement | High BHS improvement | \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Your progress is exceptional. Consider a Quarterly Business Review package\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" |\\\\\\\\n| Individual client | Client is CEO of 50+ person company | \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Your leadership team could benefit from group diagnostics\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" |\\\\\\\\n| Coaching only | Client mentions board meeting | \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"BoldEdge can generate a Board Prep Kit\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" |\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Package Bundling:\\\\\\\\n- Coach creates custom bundles: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Growth Accelerator\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" = 12 sessions + 2 diagnostics + 1 retreat + monthly KPI review\\\\\\\\n- Platform tracks usage against package\\\\\\\\n- Auto-alerts: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Client has 2 sessions remaining in their package. Renewal conversation recommended.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- Suggested pricing based on component value: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"This bundle's component value is $8,500. Recommend bundle price: $6,500-$7,500.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Seasonal Campaigns:\\\\\\\\n- **January:** \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"New Year Business Health Check\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" \\\\\\\\u2014 free diagnostic promotion for coaches to offer prospects\\\\\\\\n- **Q1:** \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Q1 Planning Workshop\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" \\\\\\\\u2014 coaches get a turnkey workshop kit (slides, workbook, diagnostic link)\\\\\\\\n- **Mid-Year:** \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Mid-Year Business Review\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" \\\\\\\\u2014 re-diagnostic push for all active clients\\\\\\\\n- **Q4:** \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Year in Review\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" \\\\\\\\u2014 auto-generated annual summary for every client\\\\\\\\n- Each campaign comes with: email templates, social media copy, workshop materials, diagnostic links\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n---\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n## 15. A coach billing $300/hr who saves 20 min/session pays for the entire month in one session prep.\n- **$349 Business** \u2192 For coaches running a real business. Financial modeling, email marketing, API, and team diagnostics justify the jump. At 25+ clients billing $500/mo avg, this is <3% of revenue.\n- **$799+ Enterprise** \u2192 White-label, multi-coach, custom everything. Priced on value, not cost. A 5-coach firm billing $50K/mo pays 1.6% for their entire technology stack.\n\n### Revenue Math\n\n| Scenario | Coaches | Mix | MRR | ARR |\n|----------|---------|-----|-----|-----|\n| Year 1 (Month 12) | 50 paying | 30% Starter, 50% Pro, 15% Biz, 5% Ent | $6,080 | $72,960 |\n| Year 2 (Month 24) | 200 paying | 25% Starter, 50% Pro, 20% Biz, 5% Ent | $27,600 | $331,200 |\n| Year 3 (Month 36) | 500 paying | 20% Starter, 45% Pro, 25% Biz, 10% Ent | $82,050 | $984,600 |\n\n**Assumptions:** Avg revenue per coach: Starter $49, Pro $149, Business $349, Enterprise $899. Weighted average ~$164/mo at scale.\n\n### Add-On Revenue Streams\n\n| Add-On | Price | Available To |\n|--------|-------|-------------|\n| Additional industry module | $29/mo each | Professional+ |\n| Business Health Certificate | $250-500/year (client pays) | Professional+ |\n| Extra 360\u00b0 rater slots (10-pack) | $19/mo | Professional+ |\n| Additional coach seat (firm) | $99/mo per seat | Business+ |\n| Data export API calls (over limit) | $0.10/call | Business+ |\n| Priority support upgrade | $49/mo | Starter/Professional |\n| Certification program enrollment | $495 one-time | All tiers |\n| Coach directory featured listing | $29/mo | All tiers |\n\n---\n\n## 2. GO-TO-MARKET LAUNCH SEQUENCE\n\n### Phase 0: Pre-Launch (Weeks -8 to -1)\n\n**Week -8 to -6: Foundation**\n- [ ] Domain registration + landing page live (boldedge.io)\n- [ ] Email capture: \"Get early access\" with value prop + 3 screenshots\n- [ ] LinkedIn personal posts from Ned (3x/week): \"Building something for coaches\"\n- [ ] Identify 20 target beta coaches (Meredith's network + LinkedIn outreach)\n- [ ] Create \"founding members\" positioning: first 20 coaches get lifetime 30% discount\n\n**Week -5 to -3: Content & Community**\n- [ ] Publish \"Why Coaching Needs a Data Revolution\" blog post\n- [ ] Create free \"Coach Health Check\" diagnostic (standalone, works without platform)\n- [ ] Share Coach Health Check on LinkedIn: \"Take a free diagnostic of your coaching business\"\n- [ ] Goal: 100 email subscribers, 50 diagnostic completions\n- [ ] Reach out personally to 20 target coaches with personalized messages\n- [ ] Create demo video (3 min): \"What BoldEdge looks like with a real client\"\n\n**Week -2 to -1: Beta Prep**\n- [ ] Platform feature-complete for MVP (core diagnostic + dashboard + sessions + tasks)\n- [ ] Meredith fully onboarded with 2-3 real clients\n- [ ] Meredith provides video testimonial + case study data\n- [ ] Onboarding flow tested and polished\n- [ ] Support documentation ready (help center + FAQ)\n- [ ] Stripe billing integration tested\n\n### Phase 1: Private Beta (Weeks 1-4)\n\n**Week 1: Meredith + 5 Coaches**\n- [ ] Meredith live with 3+ clients (existing from pre-beta)\n- [ ] 5 hand-selected coaches invited (Meredith's referrals preferred)\n- [ ] Daily check-ins with beta coaches (Slack channel or text)\n- [ ] Track: time to first client added, time to first diagnostic sent, feature usage\n- [ ] Fix critical bugs immediately\n- [ ] Coach feedback form: \"What's missing? MULTI-COACH FIRM MODE\n\n### The Opportunity\nSolo coaches are the first market, but coaching firms (5-50 coaches) represent 10x the revenue per account. BoldEdge needs a firm-level experience from Business tier up.\n\n### Firm Architecture\n\n```\n\u250c\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2510\n\u2502           FIRM ADMIN DASHBOARD          \u2502\n\u2502  (Firm owner/manager sees everything)   \u2502\n\u251c\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2524\n\u2502                                         \u2502\n\u2502  \u250c\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2510  \u250c\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2510  \u250c\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2510\n\u2502  \u2502 Coach A   \u2502  \u2502 Coach B   \u2502  \u2502 Coach \u2502\n\u2502  \u2502 12 clients\u2502  \u2502 8 clients \u2502  \u2502 C ... \u2502\n\u2502  \u2502 $14K MRR  \u2502  \u2502 $9K MRR   \u2502  \u2502       \u2502\n\u2502  \u2514\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2518  \u2514\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2518                  \u2502\n\u2502  \u2022 Total MRR: $52,000                   \u2502\n\u2502  \u2022 Avg BHS improvement: 19 points       \u2502\n\u2502  \u2022 Avg client retention: 84% Firm Admin Features\n\n**Dashboard:**\n- Aggregate metrics across all coaches: total clients, total revenue, avg BHS improvement, retention rate, session count\n- Coach comparison view (opt-in): who's most effective, who has capacity, who needs support\n- Client list with filter: by coach, by status, by risk level, by BHS range\n- Revenue tracking: MRR by coach, pipeline by coach, renewal calendar\n\n**Coach Management:**\n- Add/remove coaches (each at $99/mo per seat)\n- Assign clients to coaches (drag-and-drop or auto-assign based on fit)\n- Transfer clients between coaches (with history preserved)\n- Coach performance metrics: session adherence, task completion rates, client outcomes\n- Coach activity log: last login, sessions this week, diagnostics run\n\n**Client Assignment Logic:**\n- Manual: firm admin assigns\n- Smart match: based on coach specialty, capacity, client industry, location\n- Load balancing: \"Coach A has 15 clients, Coach C has 4. Recommend routing new clients to Coach C.\"\n- Re-assignment workflow: when a coach leaves, clients auto-queue for reassignment\n\n**Firm-Level Reporting:**\n- \"Firm Impact Report\" \u2014 quarterly aggregate: clients served, outcomes achieved, ROI generated\n- Exportable for firm marketing: \"Our firm improved average client BHS by 22 points\"\n- Individual coach reports for performance reviews\n- Client satisfaction aggregated across firm\n\n**Firm Billing:**\n- Single invoice for all seats\n- Volume discount: 5-9 seats = 10% off, 10-19 seats = 15% off, 20+ seats = 20% off\n- Coach can have individual plan OR be part of firm plan (no double-billing)\n\n### Firm Pricing\n\n| Firm Size | Base | Per Coach Seat | Total | Per Coach Savings |\n|-----------|------|---------------|-------|-------------------|\n| 2-4 coaches | $349 (Business) | $99/each additional | $547-$646 | ~$162/coach |\n| 5-9 coaches | $349 | $89/each (10% off) | $703-$1,061 | ~$118/coach |\n| 10-19 coaches | $349 | $84/each (15% off) | $1,105-$1,861 | ~$98/coach |\n| 20+ coaches | Custom | $79/each (20% off) | Custom | ~$79/coach |\n\n---\n\n## 4. CORPORATE BUYER PORTAL\n\n### The Second Market\nBeyond solo coaches and firms, there's a massive B2B market: companies buying coaching for their leaders.\n\n**Buyers:** HR directors, L&D managers, Chief People Officers, CEOs of mid-market companies (100-1,000 employees)\n\n**What They Buy:** Coaching engagements for 5-50 leaders simultaneously\n\n**What They Need (That Coaches Don't):**\n- ROI reporting rolled up across all leaders being coached\n- Compliance and confidentiality guarantees\n- Single invoice and procurement-friendly pricing\n- Progress visibility without seeing session content\n- Vendor management features\n\n### Corporate Dashboard\n\n```\n\u250c\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2510\n\u2502         ACME CORP \u2014 COACHING PROGRAM        \u2502\n\u2502  HR Admin: Sarah Johnson                     \u2502\n\u251c\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2524\n\u2502                                              \u2502\n\u2502  PROGRAM OVERVIEW                            \u2502\n\u2502  Leaders enrolled: 12                        \u2502\n\u2502  Coaches assigned: 3                         \u2502\n\u2502  Avg sessions completed: 6.2                 \u2502\n\u2502  Program start: Jan 15, 2026                 \u2502\n\u2502  Program end: Jun 15, 2026 Diagnostic completion: 100%                 \u2502\n\u2502  Session attendance: 94%                     \u2502\n\u2502  Task completion: 78%                        \u2502\n\u2502  Program NPS: 8.7/10
